# Component fixture manifest

## Break run

The run the `tested` column comes from. Session B fills these lines; `glossary.py` prints them under each
table's title, and `not run yet` for an empty one.

sha: 87463b99d83930d28df8dfecc4809be3e3a27c6e
date: 2026-11-14
users: admin (all rows); Probe Breaker (115 of the 506 deployed rows); Probe Platform (50 of the 200 in fixture rows)

## Rows

One row per Lightning UI shape. Every row sits on a page once. Lightning Experience only: Experience Cloud and
Setup are out of scope.

Status values: `new` (enumerated, not placed yet), `in fixture` (already in `test/fixture`, with its page),
`limitation` (a scratch org cannot hold it, with the reason), `deployed` (built here and on the orgs),
`NOT DEPLOYABLE` (built here, the deploy failed, with the error), `not UI` (the reference lists it, and it renders
nothing a user can see or act on, with the reason).

Tested values (a break run sets them): `driven` (sf-tester's tools worked the component), `seen` (it was seen on
the page, not worked), `no` (not tested yet). `glossary.py` ticks a box only for `driven`.

Users: the pages built here (status `deployed`, names start with `SfUiC`) open for the admin and for the break-run
user, Probe Breaker (`config/break-user-def.json`), through the permission set `SfUiC_Access`. The fixture's own
platform user (Probe Platform) holds none of this on purpose: the repo's org tests expect its limits. Where a note
says "the test user" or "the platform user" for an `SfUiC` page, read "the break-run user".

Id prefix: A1 App Builder components and pages, A2 navigation, A3 fields and forms, A4 actions, A5 lists,
A6 base components, flow screen components and overlays, A7 Aura, P a pair that a tool's code branches on (doc URL column = the
branch in src), GAP a row from `findings/manifest-gaps.md`.
